more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n Those seed evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, so the\n//   seeds of each outer iteration are distributed over the MPI ranks.\n//   Every rank keeps a full replica of the (small) point set and of the\n//   clustered/unclustered bookkeeping, so no point data has to be exchanged.\n//   Per outer iteration the ranks exchange\n//     * the per-seed cardinalities (one MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAX) over the\n//       unclustered positions), which yields the winning seed with exactly\n//       the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest\n//       cardinality, smallest point index), and\n//     * the member list of the winning cluster (one MPI_Bcast from the rank\n//       that owns the winning seed).\n//   The cardinalities of the previous iteration are also used as a cost model\n//   to statically load balance the seeds with an LPT (longest processing time\n//   first) schedule that every rank computes redundantly and identically, so\n//   the balancing itself needs no extra communication.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantically equivalent to the original code):\n//   * The maximum distance of every candidate to the current cluster is\n//     maintained incrementally, which reduces the cost of generating one\n//     candidate cluster from O(N * k^2) to O(N * k) for a cluster of size k.\n//   * All distance comparisons are performed on squared distances (monotone\n//     transformation), which removes the square root from the hot loop.\n//   * The candidate data is kept in a compact structure-of-arrays layout so\n//     the hot loop vectorizes; already-taken candidates are masked with\n//     +infinity instead of being removed.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <queue>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-rank scratch space for the candidate cluster generation.  Kept alive\n// across seeds/iterations so that no allocation happens in the hot path.\nstruct Workspace {\n    // Structure-of-arrays copy of the currently unclustered points\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy;\n    // Maximum squared distance of each candidate to the current cluster\n    std::vector<double> cmax;\n    // Global point index of each candidate\n    std::vector<int> cid;\n    // Members of the cluster that is currently being built\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\nstatic constexpr int kMinLanes = 8;  // unroll factor for the vectorized kernel\n\n// Update the incremental maxima with the newly added cluster member\n// (px, py) and return the smallest maximum found.  Candidates that are\n// already part of the cluster carry +infinity and are therefore never\n// selected again.\nstatic inline double updateAndMin(const double* __restrict cx,\n                                  const double* __restrict cy,\n                                  double* __restrict cmax,\n                                  const int n,\n                                  const double px, const double py) {\n    // Independent accumulator lanes so that the min-reduction vectorizes\n    // without requiring floating point reassociation.\n    double lane[kMinLanes];\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) lane[k] = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    int i = 0;\n    for (; i + kMinLanes <= n; i += kMinLanes) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) {\n            const double dx = cx[i + k] - px;\n            const double dy = cy[i + k] - py;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double prev = cmax[i + k];\n            const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n            cmax[i + k] = m;\n            lane[k] = m < lane[k] ? m : lane[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    double minv = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) minv = lane[k] < minv ? lane[k] : minv;\n\n    for (; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double dx = cx[i] - px;\n        const double dy = cy[i] - py;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        const double prev = cmax[i];\n        const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n        cmax[i] = m;\n        minv = m < minv ? m : minv;\n    }\n\n    return minv;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// 'ws' must hold the compacted list of all unclustered points (including the\n// seed); 'seed_pos' is the position of the seed within that list.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// ws.members (in insertion order, matching the sequential algorithm).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_pos,\n                                    const double threshold_sq,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(ws.cid.size());\n    const double inf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    std::fill(ws.cmax.begin(), ws.cmax.end(), 0.0);\n    ws.cmax[seed_pos] = inf;  // mask the seed itself\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    const int seed_point = ws.cid[seed_pos];\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    double px = ws.cx[seed_pos];\n    double py = ws.cy[seed_pos];\n\n    // Iteratively add the point that keeps the cluster diameter smallest\n    while (true) {\n        const double minv = updateAndMin(ws.cx.data(), ws.cy.data(), ws.cmax.data(),\n                                         n, px, py);\n        if (!(minv < threshold_sq)) break;  // no more points can be added\n\n        // First candidate attaining the minimum (matches the sequential\n        // code, which scans candidates in increasing point index order and\n        // only replaces the best on a strictly smaller diameter).\n        int best = 0;\n        while (ws.cmax[best] != minv) ++best;\n\n        ws.members.push_back(ws.cid[best]);\n        px = ws.cx[best];\n        py = ws.cy[best];\n        ws.cmax[best] = inf;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Distribute the seed positions [0, n_pos) over 'nranks' ranks using an LPT\n// schedule based on the given per-position cost estimate.  Deterministic, so\n// every rank obtains the identical assignment without communication.\nstatic void assignSeeds(const std::vector<int>& cost, const int nranks,\n                        std::vector<int>& owner, std::vector<int>& order) {\n    const int n_pos = static_cast<int>(cost.size());\n    owner.resize(n_pos);\n\n    order.resize(n_pos);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_pos; ++i) order[i] = i;\n    std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [&cost](int a, int b) {\n        if (cost[a] != cost[b]) return cost[a] > cost[b];\n        return a < b;\n    });\n\n    // Min-heap of (accumulated load, rank)\n    typedef std::pair<long long, int> Load;\n    std::priority_queue<Load, std::vector<Load>, std::greater<Load>> heap;\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) heap.emplace(0LL, r);\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_pos; ++k) {\n        const Load top = heap.top();\n        heap.pop();\n        const int pos = order[k];\n        owner[pos] = top.second;\n        heap.emplace(top.first + cost[pos], top.second);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel over the seed evaluations)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank, const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    const double threshold_sq = threshold * threshold;\n\n    // Replicated SoA copy of all points for the vectorized kernel\n    std::vector<double> px(N), py(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[i].x;\n        py[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;\n\n    // Cost model for the load balancing: cardinality this point produced in\n    // the previous outer iteration (1 = \"unknown\", giving a cyclic schedule).\n    std::vector<int> prev_card(N, 1);\n\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> cards, cost, owner, order, best_members, my_seeds;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n        // Compact the unclustered points into the workspace (shared by all\n        // seeds of this iteration)\n        ws.cx.resize(U);\n        ws.cy.resize(U);\n        ws.cmax.resize(U);\n        ws.cid.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            const int idx = unclustered[p];\n            ws.cid[p] = idx;\n            ws.cx[p] = px[idx];\n            ws.cy[p] = py[idx];\n        }\n\n        // Static load balanced assignment of the seeds to the ranks\n        cost.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) cost[p] = prev_card[unclustered[p]];\n        assignSeeds(cost, nranks, owner, order);\n\n        my_seeds.clear();\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (owner[p] == rank) my_seeds.push_back(p);\n        }\n\n        // Evaluate the local share of the seeds\n        cards.assign(U, 0);\n        int local_best_card = 0;\n        int local_best_pos = -1;\n        for (const int p : my_seeds) {\n            const int card = generateCandidateCluster(p, threshold_sq, ws);\n            cards[p] = card;\n            // Best local candidate: largest cardinality, smallest index\n            if (card > local_best_card) {\n                local_best_card = card;\n                local_best_pos = p;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Combine the cardinalities of all ranks.  Positions not owned by a\n        // rank contribute 0, owned ones contribute a cardinality >= 1.\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, cards.data(), U, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // Winning seed: maximum cardinality, smallest point index (the\n        // unclustered list is sorted ascending, so the first position wins).\n        int best_pos = -1;\n        int max_cardinality = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (cards[p] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cards[p];\n                best_pos = p;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_pos < 0) break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        // Fetch the member list of the winning cluster from its owner\n        best_members.resize(max_cardinality);\n        if (nranks > 1) {\n            const int root = owner[best_pos];\n            if (rank == root && local_best_pos != best_pos) {\n                // Should not happen: the local winner is the global winner\n                // whenever this rank owns it.\n                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            }\n            MPI_Bcast(best_members.data(), max_cardinality, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = unclustered[best_pos];\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n\n        // Remember the cardinalities as cost estimate for the next iteration\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) prev_card[unclustered[p]] = cards[p] > 0 ? cards[p] : 1;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    (void)rank;\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points =        MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n Those seed evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, so the\n//   seeds of each outer iteration are distributed over the MPI ranks.\n//   Every rank keeps a full replica of the (small) point set and of the\n//   clustered/unclustered bookkeeping, so no point data has to be exchanged.\n//   Per outer iteration the ranks exchange\n//     * the per-seed cardinalities (one MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAX) over the\n//       unclustered positions), which yields the winning seed with exactly\n//       the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest\n//       cardinality, smallest point index), and\n//     * the member list of the winning cluster (one MPI_Bcast from the rank\n//       that owns the winning seed).\n//   The cardinalities of the previous iteration are also used as a cost model\n//   to statically load balance the seeds with an LPT (longest processing time\n//   first) schedule that every rank computes redundantly and identically, so\n//   the balancing itself needs no extra communication.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantically equivalent to the original code):\n//   * The maximum distance of every candidate to the current cluster is\n//     maintained incrementally, which reduces the cost of generating one\n//     candidate cluster from O(N * k^2) to O(N * k) for a cluster of size k.\n//   * All distance comparisons are performed on squared distances (monotone\n//     transformation), which removes the square root from the hot loop.\n//   * The candidate data is kept in a compact structure-of-arrays layout so\n//     the hot loop vectorizes; already-taken candidates are masked with\n//     +infinity instead of being removed.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <queue>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-rank scratch space for the candidate cluster generation.  Kept alive\n// across seeds/iterations so that no allocation happens in the hot path.\nstruct Workspace {\n    // Structure-of-arrays copy of the currently unclustered points\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy;\n    // Maximum squared distance of each candidate to the current cluster\n    std::vector<double> cmax;\n    // Global point index of each candidate\n    std::vector<int> cid;\n    // Members of the cluster that is currently being built\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\nstatic constexpr int kMinLanes = 8;  // unroll factor for the vectorized kernel\n\n// Update the incremental maxima with the newly added cluster member\n// (px, py) and return the smallest maximum found.  Candidates that are\n// already part of the cluster carry +infinity and are therefore never\n// selected again.\nstatic inline double updateAndMin(const double* __restrict cx,\n                                  const double* __restrict cy,\n                                  double* __restrict cmax,\n                                  const int n,\n                                  const double px, const double py) {\n    // Independent accumulator lanes so that the min-reduction vectorizes\n    // without requiring floating point reassociation.\n    double lane[kMinLanes];\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) lane[k] = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    int i = 0;\n    for (; i + kMinLanes <= n; i += kMinLanes) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) {\n            const double dx = cx[i + k] - px;\n            const double dy = cy[i + k] - py;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double prev = cmax[i + k];\n            const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n            cmax[i + k] = m;\n            lane[k] = m < lane[k] ? m : lane[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    double minv = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) minv = lane[k] < minv ? lane[k] : minv;\n\n    for (; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double dx = cx[i] - px;\n        const double dy = cy[i] - py;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        const double prev = cmax[i];\n        const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n        cmax[i] = m;\n        minv = m < minv ? m : minv;\n    }\n\n    return minv;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// 'ws' must hold the compacted list of all unclustered points (including the\n// seed); 'seed_pos' is the position of the seed within that list.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// ws.members (in insertion order, matching the sequential algorithm).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_pos,\n                                    const double threshold_sq,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(ws.cid.size());\n    const double inf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    std::fill(ws.cmax.begin(), ws.cmax.end(), 0.0);\n    ws.cmax[seed_pos] = inf;  // mask the seed itself\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    const int seed_point = ws.cid[seed_pos];\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    double px = ws.cx[seed_pos];\n    double py = ws.cy[seed_pos];\n\n    // Iteratively add the point that keeps the cluster diameter smallest\n    while (true) {\n        const double minv = updateAndMin(ws.cx.data(), ws.cy.data(), ws.cmax.data(),\n                                         n, px, py);\n        if (!(minv < threshold_sq)) break;  // no more points can be added\n\n        // First candidate attaining the minimum (matches the sequential\n        // code, which scans candidates in increasing point index order and\n        // only replaces the best on a strictly smaller diameter).\n        int best = 0;\n        while (ws.cmax[best] != minv) ++best;\n\n        ws.members.push_back(ws.cid[best]);\n        px = ws.cx[best];\n        py = ws.cy[best];\n        ws.cmax[best] = inf;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Distribute the seed positions [0, n_pos) over 'nranks' ranks using an LPT\n// schedule based on the given per-position cost estimate.  Deterministic, so\n// every rank obtains the identical assignment without communication.\nstatic void assignSeeds(const std::vector<int>& cost, const int nranks,\n                        std::vector<int>& owner, std::vector<int>& order) {\n    const int n_pos = static_cast<int>(cost.size());\n    owner.resize(n_pos);\n\n    order.resize(n_pos);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_pos; ++i) order[i] = i;\n    std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [&cost](int a, int b) {\n        if (cost[a] != cost[b]) return cost[a] > cost[b];\n        return a < b;\n    });\n\n    // Min-heap of (accumulated load, rank)\n    typedef std::pair<long long, int> Load;\n    std::priority_queue<Load, std::vector<Load>, std::greater<Load>> heap;\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) heap.emplace(0LL, r);\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_pos; ++k) {\n        const Load top = heap.top();\n        heap.pop();\n        const int pos = order[k];\n        owner[pos] = top.second;\n        heap.emplace(top.first + cost[pos], top.second);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel over the seed evaluations)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank, const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    const double threshold_sq = threshold * threshold;\n\n    // Replicated SoA copy of all points for the vectorized kernel\n    std::vector<double> px(N), py(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[i].x;\n        py[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;\n\n    // Cost model for the load balancing: cardinality this point produced in\n    // the previous outer iteration (1 = \"unknown\", giving a cyclic schedule).\n    std::vector<int> prev_card(N, 1);\n\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> cards, cost, owner, order, best_members, my_seeds;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n        // Compact the unclustered points into the workspace (shared by all\n        // seeds of this iteration)\n        ws.cx.resize(U);\n        ws.cy.resize(U);\n        ws.cmax.resize(U);\n        ws.cid.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            const int idx = unclustered[p];\n            ws.cid[p] = idx;\n            ws.cx[p] = px[idx];\n            ws.cy[p] = py[idx];\n        }\n\n        // Static load balanced assignment of the seeds to the ranks\n        cost.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) cost[p] = prev_card[unclustered[p]];\n        assignSeeds(cost, nranks, owner, order);\n\n        my_seeds.clear();\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (owner[p] == rank) my_seeds.push_back(p);\n        }\n\n        // Evaluate the local share of the seeds\n        cards.assign(U, 0);\n        int local_best_card = 0;\n        int local_best_pos = -1;\n        for (const int p : my_seeds) {\n            const int card = generateCandidateCluster(p, threshold_sq, ws);\n            cards[p] = card;\n            // Best local candidate: largest cardinality, smallest index\n            if (card > local_best_card) {\n                local_best_card = card;\n                local_best_pos = p;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Combine the cardinalities of all ranks.  Positions not owned by a\n        // rank contribute 0, owned ones contribute a cardinality >= 1.\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, cards.data(), U, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // Winning seed: maximum cardinality, smallest point index (the\n        // unclustered list is sorted ascending, so the first position wins).\n        int best_pos = -1;\n        int max_cardinality = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (cards[p] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cards[p];\n                best_pos = p;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_pos < 0) break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        // Fetch the member list of the winning cluster from its owner\n        best_members.resize(max_cardinality);\n        if (nranks > 1) {\n            const int root = owner[best_pos];\n            if (rank == root && local_best_pos != best_pos) {\n                // Should not happen: the local winner is the global winner\n                // whenever this rank owns it.\n                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            }\n            MPI_Bcast(best_members.data(), max_cardinality, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = unclustered[best_pos];\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n\n        // Remember the cardinalities as cost estimate for the next iteration\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) prev_card[unclustered[p]] = cards[p] > 0 ? cards[p] : 1;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    (void)rank;\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n                   num_points, threshold);\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n        printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", nranks);\n    }\n\n    // Generate synthetic data (identical on every rank: generated on rank 0\n    // and broadcast so that all ranks see bit-identical coordinates)\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(points.data(), 2 * num_points, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, rank, nranks);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    if (rank != 0) {\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n           total_clustered, num_points,\n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int exit_code = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            exit_code = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exit_code;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":8,"newStart":1,"newLines":37,"lines":["-// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E6yNip7enGShwqW3M5wWdf","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r1/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a9d1acbc-8b20-46a4-8c52-31a231c673e3","uuid":"e8920f33-0f4c-4080-8898-bba80fa957a0","timestamp":"2026-09-02T17:46:17.429Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r1/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","oldString":"    (void)rank;\n    return clusters;","newString":"    return clusters;","originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy (MPI, distributed memory):\n//   The dominant cost of the algorithm is the \"candidate cluster\" generation\n//   that is performed independently for every still-unclustered point acting\n//   as a seed.  Those seed evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, so the\n//   seeds of each outer iteration are distributed over the MPI ranks.\n//   Every rank keeps a full replica of the (small) point set and of the\n//   clustered/unclustered bookkeeping, so no point data has to be exchanged.\n//   Per outer iteration the ranks exchange\n//     * the per-seed cardinalities (one MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAX) over the\n//       unclustered positions), which yields the winning seed with exactly\n//       the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest\n//       cardinality, smallest point index), and\n//     * the member list of the winning cluster (one MPI_Bcast from the rank\n//       that owns the winning seed).\n//   The cardinalities of the previous iteration are also used as a cost model\n//   to statically load balance the seeds with an LPT (longest processing time\n//   first) schedule that every rank computes redundantly and identically, so\n//   the balancing itself needs no extra communication.\n//\n// Serial optimizations (semantically equivalent to the original code):\n//   * The maximum distance of every candidate to the current cluster is\n//     maintained incrementally, which reduces the cost of generating one\n//     candidate cluster from O(N * k^2) to O(N * k) for a cluster of size k.\n//   * All distance comparisons are performed on squared distances (monotone\n//     transformation), which removes the square root from the hot loop.\n//   * The candidate data is kept in a compact structure-of-arrays layout so\n//     the hot loop vectorizes; already-taken candidates are masked with\n//     +infinity instead of being removed.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <queue>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-rank scratch space for the candidate cluster generation.  Kept alive\n// across seeds/iterations so that no allocation happens in the hot path.\nstruct Workspace {\n    // Structure-of-arrays copy of the currently unclustered points\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy;\n    // Maximum squared distance of each candidate to the current cluster\n    std::vector<double> cmax;\n    // Global point index of each candidate\n    std::vector<int> cid;\n    // Members of the cluster that is currently being built\n    std::vector<int> members;\n};\n\nstatic constexpr int kMinLanes = 8;  // unroll factor for the vectorized kernel\n\n// Update the incremental maxima with the newly added cluster member\n// (px, py) and return the smallest maximum found.  Candidates that are\n// already part of the cluster carry +infinity and are therefore never\n// selected again.\nstatic inline double updateAndMin(const double* __restrict cx,\n                                  const double* __restrict cy,\n                                  double* __restrict cmax,\n                                  const int n,\n                                  const double px, const double py) {\n    // Independent accumulator lanes so that the min-reduction vectorizes\n    // without requiring floating point reassociation.\n    double lane[kMinLanes];\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) lane[k] = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    int i = 0;\n    for (; i + kMinLanes <= n; i += kMinLanes) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) {\n            const double dx = cx[i + k] - px;\n            const double dy = cy[i + k] - py;\n            const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const double prev = cmax[i + k];\n            const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n            cmax[i + k] = m;\n            lane[k] = m < lane[k] ? m : lane[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    double minv = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n    for (int k = 0; k < kMinLanes; ++k) minv = lane[k] < minv ? lane[k] : minv;\n\n    for (; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double dx = cx[i] - px;\n        const double dy = cy[i] - py;\n        const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n        const double prev = cmax[i];\n        const double m = d2 > prev ? d2 : prev;\n        cmax[i] = m;\n        minv = m < minv ? m : minv;\n    }\n\n    return minv;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// 'ws' must hold the compacted list of all unclustered points (including the\n// seed); 'seed_pos' is the position of the seed within that list.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// ws.members (in insertion order, matching the sequential algorithm).\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_pos,\n                                    const double threshold_sq,\n                                    Workspace& ws) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(ws.cid.size());\n    const double inf = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n\n    std::fill(ws.cmax.begin(), ws.cmax.end(), 0.0);\n    ws.cmax[seed_pos] = inf;  // mask the seed itself\n\n    ws.members.clear();\n    const int seed_point = ws.cid[seed_pos];\n    ws.members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    double px = ws.cx[seed_pos];\n    double py = ws.cy[seed_pos];\n\n    // Iteratively add the point that keeps the cluster diameter smallest\n    while (true) {\n        const double minv = updateAndMin(ws.cx.data(), ws.cy.data(), ws.cmax.data(),\n                                         n, px, py);\n        if (!(minv < threshold_sq)) break;  // no more points can be added\n\n        // First candidate attaining the minimum (matches the sequential\n        // code, which scans candidates in increasing point index order and\n        // only replaces the best on a strictly smaller diameter).\n        int best = 0;\n        while (ws.cmax[best] != minv) ++best;\n\n        ws.members.push_back(ws.cid[best]);\n        px = ws.cx[best];\n        py = ws.cy[best];\n        ws.cmax[best] = inf;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(ws.members.size());\n}\n\n// Distribute the seed positions [0, n_pos) over 'nranks' ranks using an LPT\n// schedule based on the given per-position cost estimate.  Deterministic, so\n// every rank obtains the identical assignment without communication.\nstatic void assignSeeds(const std::vector<int>& cost, const int nranks,\n                        std::vector<int>& owner, std::vector<int>& order) {\n    const int n_pos = static_cast<int>(cost.size());\n    owner.resize(n_pos);\n\n    order.resize(n_pos);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_pos; ++i) order[i] = i;\n    std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [&cost](int a, int b) {\n        if (cost[a] != cost[b]) return cost[a] > cost[b];\n        return a < b;\n    });\n\n    // Min-heap of (accumulated load, rank)\n    typedef std::pair<long long, int> Load;\n    std::priority_queue<Load, std::vector<Load>, std::greater<Load>> heap;\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) heap.emplace(0LL, r);\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < n_pos; ++k) {\n        const Load top = heap.top();\n        heap.pop();\n        const int pos = order[k];\n        owner[pos] = top.second;\n        heap.emplace(top.first + cost[pos], top.second);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI parallel over the seed evaluations)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank, const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    const double threshold_sq = threshold * threshold;\n\n    // Replicated SoA copy of all points for the vectorized kernel\n    std::vector<double> px(N), py(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        px[i] = points[i].x;\n        py[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;\n\n    // Cost model for the load balancing: cardinality this point produced in\n    // the previous outer iteration (1 = \"unknown\", giving a cyclic schedule).\n    std::vector<int> prev_card(N, 1);\n\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Workspace ws;\n    std::vector<int> cards, cost, owner, order, best_members, my_seeds;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered.size());\n\n        // Compact the unclustered points into the workspace (shared by all\n        // seeds of this iteration)\n        ws.cx.resize(U);\n        ws.cy.resize(U);\n        ws.cmax.resize(U);\n        ws.cid.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            const int idx = unclustered[p];\n            ws.cid[p] = idx;\n            ws.cx[p] = px[idx];\n            ws.cy[p] = py[idx];\n        }\n\n        // Static load balanced assignment of the seeds to the ranks\n        cost.resize(U);\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) cost[p] = prev_card[unclustered[p]];\n        assignSeeds(cost, nranks, owner, order);\n\n        my_seeds.clear();\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (owner[p] == rank) my_seeds.push_back(p);\n        }\n\n        // Evaluate the local share of the seeds\n        cards.assign(U, 0);\n        int local_best_card = 0;\n        int local_best_pos = -1;\n        for (const int p : my_seeds) {\n            const int card = generateCandidateCluster(p, threshold_sq, ws);\n            cards[p] = card;\n            // Best local candidate: largest cardinality, smallest index\n            if (card > local_best_card) {\n                local_best_card = card;\n                local_best_pos = p;\n                best_members = ws.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Combine the cardinalities of all ranks.  Positions not owned by a\n        // rank contribute 0, owned ones contribute a cardinality >= 1.\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, cards.data(), U, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        // Winning seed: maximum cardinality, smallest point index (the\n        // unclustered list is sorted ascending, so the first position wins).\n        int best_pos = -1;\n        int max_cardinality = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            if (cards[p] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cards[p];\n                best_pos = p;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_pos < 0) break;  // No more clusters can be formed\n\n        // Fetch the member list of the winning cluster from its owner\n        best_members.resize(max_cardinality);\n        if (nranks > 1) {\n            const int root = owner[best_pos];\n            if (rank == root && local_best_pos != best_pos) {\n                // Should not happen: the local winner is the global winner\n                // whenever this rank owns it.\n                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n            }\n            MPI_Bcast(best_members.data(), max_cardinality, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = unclustered[best_pos];\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n\n        // Remember the cardinalities as cost estimate for the next iteration\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) prev_card[unclustered[p]] = cards[p] > 0 ? cards[p] : 1;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered.begin(), unclustered.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    (void)rank;\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool        MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cef2kvznT6jdE1srP2X1d","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Clean build (no warnings), correct output, tree restored to just the two source files.\n\n## What I did\n\n**MPI parallelization** (`qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp`) — the outer loop is inherently sequential (each cluster removes points before the next round), so the parallelism is in the seed evaluations inside each round, which are independent:\n\n- Every rank keeps a full replica of the point set and the clustered/unclustered bookkeeping, so no point data is ever exchanged after the initial `MPI_Bcast` of the generated data.\n- Each round, the unclustered seeds are partitioned across ranks; each rank evaluates its share.\n- Two collectives per round: one `MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAX)` over the per-seed cardinality array (positions a rank doesn't own contribute 0), and one `MPI_Bcast` of the winning cluster's member list from its owner. Deriving the winner from the reduced cardinality array reproduces the original tie-break exactly — largest cardinality, smallest point index — because the unclustered list stays sorted ascending.\n- **Load balancing:** per-seed cost varies with the cluster size it produces, so seeds are assigned by an LPT (longest-processing-time-first) schedule using the previous round's cardinalities as the cost model. Every rank computes the identical schedule redundantly, so balancing costs no extra communication and the Bcast root is known to all ranks without an extra exchange. Measured ~10% faster than plain cyclic at n=8000.\n\n**Serial kernel optimizations** (semantics preserved):\n- The max distance from each candidate to the cluster is maintained incrementally rather than recomputed, taking one candidate-cluster generation from O(N·k²) to O(N·k).\n- All comparisons on squared distances (monotone), removing `sqrt` from the hot loop.\n- Structure-of-arrays candidate layout with taken candidates masked by `+inf` instead of compacted out, so the update+min-reduction loop is branch-free and vectorizes (manually laned to avoid needing FP reassociation).\n\n**CMakeLists.txt**: `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` + link `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Same executable name, no new dependencies, no new files.\n\n## Verification\n\nFull output (including validation block and result hash) is byte-identical to the original for n=300/t=1.5, n=700/t=3.0, n=1000/t=2.0, n=2000/t=2.0 at 1, 2, 4, 7, 16, 32, 128 and 256 ranks; validation PASSES everywhere.\n\n| | orig | 1 rank | 8 | 32 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| n=2000 | 88.6 s | 2.27 s | 0.30 s | 0.092 s | 0.043 s |\n| n=8000 | — | — | 17.8 s | 4.52 s | 1.26 s |\n\nThe serial rewrite alone is 39× on n=2000; MPI adds 14.1× going from 8→128 ranks on n=8000 (88% efficiency over 16× the ranks). At n=2000 the 128-rank efficiency drops to ~41%, which is the small-problem limit — only ~16 seeds per rank and 135 collective rounds.\n\nOne equivalence caveat worth stating: comparing squared distances instead of `sqrt`-ed ones can in principle differ from the original when a candidate's distance sits within ~1 ulp of the threshold (or two candidates' distances round to the same double). each rank evaluates its share.\n- Two collectives per round: one `MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAX)` over the per-seed cardinality array (positions a rank doesn't own contribute 0), and one `MPI_Bcast` of the winning cluster's member list from its owner. Deriving the winner from the reduced cardinality array reproduces the original tie-break exactly — largest cardinality, smallest point index — because the unclustered list stays sorted ascending.\n- **Load balancing:** per-seed cost varies with the cluster size it produces, so seeds are assigned by an LPT (longest-processing-time-first) schedule using the previous round's cardinalities as the cost model. Every rank computes the identical schedule redundantly, so balancing costs no extra communication and the Bcast root is known to all ranks without an extra exchange. Measured ~10% faster than plain cyclic at n=8000.\n\n**Serial kernel optimizations** (semantics preserved):\n- The max distance from each candidate to the cluster is maintained incrementally rather than recomputed, taking one candidate-cluster generation from O(N·k²) to O(N·k).\n- All comparisons on squared distances (monotone), removing `sqrt` from the hot loop.\n- Structure-of-arrays candidate layout with taken candidates masked by `+inf` instead of compacted out, so the update+min-reduction loop is branch-free and vectorizes (manually laned to avoid needing FP reassociation).\n\n**CMakeLists.txt**: `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` + link `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Same executable name, no new dependencies, no new files.\n\n## Verification\n\nFull output (including validation block and result hash) is byte-identical to the original for n=300/t=1.5, n=700/t=3.0, n=1000/t=2.0, n=2000/t=2.0 at 1, 2, 4, 7, 16, 32, 128 and 256 ranks; validation PASSES everywhere.\n\n| | orig | 1 rank | 8 | 32 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| n=2000 | 88.6 s | 2.27 s | 0.30 s | 0.092 s | 0.043 s |\n| n=8000 | — | — | 17.8 s | 4.52 s | 1.26 s |\n\nThe serial rewrite alone is 39× on n=2000; MPI adds 14.1× going from 8→128 ranks on n=8000 (88% efficiency over 16× the ranks). At n=2000 the 128-rank efficiency drops to ~41%, which is the small-problem limit — only ~16 seeds per rank and 135 collective rounds.\n\nOne equivalence caveat worth stating: comparing squared distances instead of `sqrt`-ed ones can in principle differ from the original when a candidate's distance sits within ~1 ulp of the threshold (or two candidates' distances round to the same double).
